# Upstream sync report — 2026-09-01, target `cdefd9d`

Wave `SYNCENUM`, second pass. Steps 1 to 4 of the cycle in
[`../upstream-sync.md`](../upstream-sync.md), redone against the **complete**
upstream history. This report ports nothing and does not move the pin.

Issue: [#2490](https://github.com/mudler/vllm.cpp/issues/2490).

This is a **new report rather than a revision** of
[`2026-09-01-adebc41.md`](2026-09-01-adebc41.md). The target SHA changed, and
the report file name keys on the target, so a revision in place would have made
the file name lie. Keeping both also keeps the provenance readable: the first
report is where the shallow-checkout defect was found and dated, and it should
stay legible as that. It now carries a `SUPERSEDED IN PART` note pointing here.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Active pin | `5559679229bc961848b121ccdeaa8fa5d79bec98`, 2026-07-26 |
| Target | `cdefd9d4997f00da72dc6245cc60678b50761b7e`, 2026-09-02 00:53 +0900 |
| Target subject | `[Bugfix] Support Sentence Transformers 5.4+ serialized configs (#54533)` |
| Reference checkout | `${VLLM_SOURCE}`, `origin` = `https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm` |
| Fetched | 2026-09-01, `git fetch origin main`, exit 0 |
| Pin moved | NO. The cycle stops after step 4. |

`origin/main` moved twice while this cycle ran: `adebc41b7e` during the first
pass, then `f1e5fdd7f2` and `cdefd9d499` during the unshallow. `adebc41b7e` is an
ancestor of `cdefd9d499`, and the eight commits between them are enumerated here.

## 1. The shallow defect is resolved, and the real delta is 2.6x larger

The first pass found that the reference checkout was a shallow repository whose
`.git/shallow` grafted `41f179b57a` (2026-08-18) as an artificial root. The
developer authorised `git fetch --unshallow origin` and it completed.

```console
$ git -C ${VLLM_SOURCE} rev-parse --is-shallow-repository
false
$ ls ${VLLM_SOURCE}/.git/shallow
ls: cannot access '.git/shallow': No such file or directory
$ git -C ${VLLM_SOURCE} merge-base 5559679229 cdefd9d499
5559679229bc961848b121ccdeaa8fa5d79bec98
$ git -C ${VLLM_SOURCE} rev-list --count 5559679229..cdefd9d499
1566
$ git -C ${VLLM_SOURCE} rev-list --count cdefd9d499
20692
```

The pin is its own merge base with the target, which is the definition of an
ancestor. The range is **1566 commits, not the 594 the wave started from and not
the 595 the truncated range printed**. The truncated history showed 20692 - 595
commits fewer than exist. Measured against the first pass's own target:

| Range | Shallow count | True count | Hidden |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `555967922..adebc41b7e` | 595 | 1558 | 963 |
| `adebc41b7e..cdefd9d499` | n/a | 8 | 0 |
| `555967922..cdefd9d499` | n/a | 1566 | — |

**The invisible window is now visible.** The first report recorded 212
already-ported files changing across it with +19509 and -6591 lines and no commit
to attribute them to. Those 963 commits are the attribution, and they are
classified in §4 to §6 of this report.

**`41f179b57a` (carry-over C2) is resolved and is not an UNCLASSIFIED item.** It
now has a parent, `eab1cff5b0`, and its real diff is 19 files: the Rust frontend
crate under `rust/src/server/`, one example and one test. It touches **no**
mirrored path, so it correctly falls outside the classified universe rather than
inside it as an unclassifiable entry. Its apparent 6619-file, 1939079-insertion
diff was the shallow boundary presenting its whole tree.

## 2. Corrections to the first report

Three statements in [`2026-09-01-adebc41.md`](2026-09-01-adebc41.md) were caused
by the truncated history and are corrected here. The dispositions in that
report's §4 to §6 are **not** among them: a commit's diff is fixed by its SHA,
and every commit except the graft root had its true parent present in the shallow
clone, so those 429 diffs were the real diffs and those classifications are
carried forward unchanged.

**Correction 1, and it is a factual error, not a bound.** The first report's §8
says `ad5d29db7` "is not an ancestor of the target, for the same squash reason".
That is false. `ad5d29db70` **is** an ancestor of the target, dated 2026-07-29,
three days after the pin. The object was unreachable in a truncated history, and
a `merge-base --is-ancestor` against an unreachable object answers no for a
reason that has nothing to do with squash merges. Deviation 17's anchor was
correct all along.

**Correction 2.** The first report could not determine which upstream commit
merged Muse Glimmer, `dots3_note` or the Qwen3.5 text-only arms. All three are
findable now, and they discharge three of the four deviation conflicts. See §7.

**Correction 3.** The first report's §7 put twelve cited paths in a group called
"anchors that name no upstream file at either revision, so they cannot be
verified against anything", and listed them as carry-over C5. With the full
history, **nine of the twelve are stale-but-real anchors**: they existed
upstream and were removed or moved before the pin. Only three are genuinely
wrong. See §8.

The squash-merge reasoning the first report applied to `075d645af6` remains
correct. That SHA is the Muse Glimmer PR branch head, subject
`Remove MUSE_GLIMMER_README.md`, and it is genuinely not an ancestor of the
target; the merged squash is `6adad08767`.

## 3. Method, and what was reused

The method of the first report is unchanged and is not re-derived here. The 369
mirrored upstream paths come from the same command, and they resolve to the same
369 against the pin and this target:

```console
$ grep -rhoE 'vllm/[A-Za-z0-9_/]+\.py' src/ include/ tools/ | sort -u   # 385 cited
$ comm -12 <cited> <(git ls-tree -r --name-only 5559679229 -- vllm/; \
                     git ls-tree -r --name-only cdefd9d499 -- vllm/ | sort -u)
369
```

Tier A is the commits touching one of those 369 files. Tier B is the commits
touching one of the 99 mirrored directories or 10 mirrored root modules without
touching a ported file. Over the true range:

| Tier | Commits |
|---|---:|
| A, touches a ported file | 627 |
| B, touches a mirrored subtree only | 450 |
| **Mirrored-surface universe** | **1077** |
| Touches no mirrored path | 489 |
| **Range total** | **1566** |

**429 of the 1077 were already classified in the first pass and are reused
verbatim.** The remaining **648** were classified in this pass, 369 in Tier A and
279 in Tier B, by twelve fresh readers working from the same brief against the
now-complete history.

`vllm/models/` is still a **parallel home** to `vllm/model_executor/models/`, not
a completed migration. At the pin it held `deepseek_v32`, `deepseek_v4`,
`inkling` and `minimax_m3`; at the target it also holds `common`, `dots3_note`,
`hy_v4`, `kimi_k3` and `qwen4_exp`, while `vllm/model_executor/models/` remains
the larger directory. Both were enumerated. A sweep assuming the old layout would
have missed `qwen4_exp` entirely, which is the commit this cycle exists for.

### Result

| Disposition | Commits | (first pass, shallow) |
|---|---:|---:|
| PORT-NOW | 315 | 122 |
| INVENTORY | 356 | 125 |
| IGNORE | 406 | 182 |
| UNCLASSIFIED | **0** | 1 |
| **Total classified** | **1077** | 430 |

**Nothing is unclassified.** The first pass's single UNCLASSIFIED entry was the
shallow-graft root, and §1 shows it falls outside the universe entirely.

Two mechanical checks ran over the finished lists, the same two as the first
pass, now over 1077 commits instead of 430:

```console
$ comm -23 classified.sha universe.sha   # classified but never enumerated -> empty
$ comm -13 classified.sha universe.sha   # enumerated but never classified -> empty
$ uniq -d classified.sha                  # duplicates -> empty
$ for each line: git log -1 --format=%s <sha> must contain "(#NNNNN)"  -> 0 mismatches
```

A third check validates the reuse of the 429 first-pass classifications. For
every reused commit the ported-file numstat was recomputed under the complete
history and compared byte-for-byte with the value recorded under the shallow
clone:

```text
reused shas: 429
tier-A entries comparable: 258
MISMATCHED numstat under full history: 0
```

Every reused diff is identical to the diff that was classified. Only the graft
root's diff was wrong, and that commit is not reused. The reuse is therefore
mechanically justified rather than assumed.

## 4. PORT-NOW (315)

Each entry changes the runtime behavior of an upstream file this tree has
already ported. The list is the work queue for step 5. It is ordered by upstream
commit SHA, which carries no priority; §11 names the items to take first.

> **MEASURED 2026-09-01 by the PINPORT wave (#2524): this count is an upper
> bound on the QUEUE, not a count of code gaps.** Every disposition here was
> derived by reading the upstream diff. None was read against this tree, which
> §10 C7 already records. **Two stratified samples totalling 63 of the 315
> entries** (every tenth, at offsets three and six) were re-derived against the
> tree at `63889449c` by seven independent readers. The result:
> **11 REAL_GAP, 12 ALREADY_SATISFIED, 40 NOT_APPLICABLE, 0 UNCERTAIN**. About
> one entry in six is portable work today, and the two samples agree closely
> (5 of 32, then 6 of 31), so the rate is stable rather than one unlucky draw.
> Point estimate for the whole queue: **roughly 55 real gaps out of 315.**
>
> C7 names one failure shape, an upstream API widening whose behavior lands in
> an unported file. The sample found two more that C7 does not name, and they
> are the bulk of the 23. **The surface is absent**: the commit edits something
> this tree deliberately does not carry, so there is nothing to port until the
> underlying feature is (upstream's own benchmark harness, the ZMQ and Rust
> frontends, `AutoWeightsLoader`, torch tensor placement, Gumbel draft
> sampling). **The surface is present but the delta is inert here**: the tree
> has the function the commit edits, but the new arm is gated on a backend or a
> spec field that is unreachable in every configuration this tree can build
> (`FLASHMLA_SPARSE`, FA4, SM100, `flashkda`, DCP). The second kind is deferred
> work rather than discarded work: it becomes real the day that gate is ported.
> A third shape appeared as well, an upstream DEFECT FIX whose defect this
> tree's structure makes impossible, so the fix has no analogue.
>
> The eleven real gaps are listed with their evidence in §13, which is the
> worked list the next wave should port from. Two of them are LIVE DEFECTS on
> reachable paths rather than missing forward-ports: `4d341ca829` vllm#49206
> (now [#2527](https://github.com/mudler/vllm.cpp/issues/2527)) and `a0c092ee72`
> vllm#48245 (now [#2531](https://github.com/mudler/vllm.cpp/issues/2531)).
>
> **No entry below is reclassified on the strength of a sample.** The estimate
> justifies re-deriving the queue against the tree before a wave works it; it
> does not license deleting entries. See C10.

- `00f7f25828` vllm#53127 [Misc] Don't allow language-model-only used with encoder CG together — PORT-NOW: vllm/config/vllm.py adds a new ValueError when language_model_only meets cudagraph_mm_encoder; new config-validation behavior.
- `017e9f4448` vllm#52216 Promote prefix_cache_retention_interval to an argument and change the default to 0 — PORT-NOW: config/cache.py, arg_utils.py, kv_cache_interface.py, kv_cache_coordinator.py and kv_cache_utils.py move the retention interval from an env to a resolved KVCacheConfig field and change its default from None to 0, changing which sliding-window and Mamba prefix-cache checkpoints are kept.
- `01e56caaf2` vllm#52512 [Bugfix][MLA] Do not use Dense MHA for GLM-5.2 — PORT-NOW: mla_attention.py adds a layer-level supports_dense_mha_prefill class var and ANDs it into the prefill-backend selection; changes which prefill path a sparse MLA layer takes.
- `021b7d985b` vllm#49390 [Perf] Raise Blackwell CUDA graph capture default to 1024 — PORT-NOW: config/vllm.py _set_cudagraph_sizes default cap becomes 1024 on device-capability family 100 instead of a flat 512; vllm.cpp mirrors this formula in include/vllm/model_executor/models/decode_graph_sizes.h, which still hardcodes the 512 cap.
  **CORRECTED 2026-09-03 (PORTQ-3, #2647): the second clause of this entry is FALSE about this tree.** `include/vllm/model_executor/models/decode_graph_sizes.h` does not hardcode a 512 cap and applies no cap at all; `DecodeGraphSizes` bounds its candidate set by `max_num_seqs`. `512` occurs once in that file, inside a comment describing upstream's formula, and `1024` does not occur. Upstream raising the cap cannot change a set already bounded below it. Read against the tree three times independently before this annotation — #2524's stratified sample, `2026-09-02-db92053.md:162-176`, and `2026-09-03-portq3.md` §6.2 — and none of the three reached this line, so a fourth wave would have re-derived it. The disposition is NOT_APPLICABLE. The reading of the UPSTREAM diff above is accurate and is left as written.

There is no field and no counting kernel, so the disposition is NOT_APPLICABLE (surface-absent) rather than PORT-NOW. See `2026-09-03-portq3.md` §3 [94] and §5 Shape A. The reading of the UPSTREAM diff above is accurate and is left as written.

- `ef47a897e2` vllm#51875 [Core] Make prefix-cache NONE_HASH deterministic by default — PORT-NOW: v1/core/kv_cache_utils.py derives NONE_HASH from a fixed DEFAULT_NONE_HASH_SEED for cryptographic hash functions (random only for xxhash) and exposes get_none_hash_seed; the prefix-cache block hash values change by default.
  **CORRECTED 2026-09-03 (PORTQ-5, #2679): the final clause is FALSE about this tree.** Block hash values here do NOT change, because they are already deterministic by default and have been since before this commit. `init_none_hash` (`src/vllm/v1/core/kv_cache_utils.cpp:376-412`) resolves explicit arg > `$VLLM_PREFIX_CACHING_HASH_SEED` > `$PYTHONHASHSEED` > the fixed `kDefaultNoneHashSeed` (`include/vllm/v1/core/kv_cache_utils.h:226`), and `include/vllm/v1/core/kv_cache_utils.h:216-237` records the deviation in as many words: upstream fell back to `os.urandom(32)` and "our threat model has no hash-DoS concern, so the deterministic value is the DEFAULT and randomness is the opt-in". It is reached in production through `LoadedEngine::EnsureNoneHash()` (`src/vllm/entrypoints/model_loader.cpp:1392-1405`, called from the member-init list at `:2119`), and gated by `tests/vllm/v1/test_none_hash_determinism.cpp`. Upstream's `get_none_hash_seed()` is answered by `none_hash_provenance()` (`include/vllm/v1/core/kv_cache_utils.h:207-214`). The disposition is ALREADY_SATISFIED: this tree converged on the behaviour BEFORE upstream did, so at the pin upstream is the side that is behind. Two residuals, neither of them this commit's: the seed literal differs (`"vllm.cpp/none_hash/v1"` vs `"vllm-none-hash"`), which the header forbids changing and which `src/vllm/v1/kv_offload/cache_identity.cpp:49,107-108` already detects rather than silently mis-hits; and upstream's non-cryptographic (`xxhash`) branch has no surface here because `sha256_cbor` is the only shipped hasher. The reading of the UPSTREAM diff above is accurate and is left as written. `.agents/sync/2026-09-01-adebc41.md:946` repeats the same false clause and is annotated there too.

So the net upstream delta across the pin-to-target range for that file is ZERO, and hashing pin-vs-target for every path the revert touches leaves `eager_scratch.py`, the fused qnorm/rope/kv-insert kernel and `sparse_attn_compress_cutedsl.py` byte-IDENTICAL. Neither entry is portable work under ANY reading of this tree, and a wave that meets only one of the two would be reading a phantom delta. This tree also never ported either side (`eager_scratch_pool` -> 0 in `src/`+`include/`+`tests/`; the `ScratchPool` symbols that do exist are the unrelated FA2 and GDN per-stream pools at `src/vt/cuda/cuda_flash_attn_fa2.cu:259` and `src/vt/cuda/cuda_gdn.cu:5013`). Both dispositions are NOT_APPLICABLE surface-absent; PORTQ-2 independently reached that label for `df71917cf1` at its entry 45 without seeing the pairing. A second such pair exists in the range and is NOT both in this list: `aeeb36b1f1` vllm#50000 adds the DSpark-plus-DCP refusal that `d1e3eee6fb` (entry 185) deletes. The readings of the UPSTREAM diffs above are accurate and are left as written.

  **CORRECTED 2026-09-03 (PORTQ-7, #2717): this entry and `9ff7041b55` above are the SAME CHANGE, and both are in this queue.** `9ff7041b55` vllm#53002 (queue position 229) and `f620499ee3` vllm#53336 (position 241) are BYTE-IDENTICAL: same four files, same `--stat` (70 insertions, 18 deletions), and `diff` of their sorted signed line multisets from `git show -U0` is EMPTY. A scan over all 290 for `A.plus == B.plus && A.minus == B.minus` returns this pair and no other, so it is the queue's only duplicate. What sits between them is `8bdc70ec7b` vllm#51718 (position 230), KV-layout stage 6, which was branched before vllm#53002 landed and deleted 66 of 66 of its added lines on merge (measured as `git show -U0`, sign stripped, blank lines dropped, no comment filter and no de-duplication -- an earlier draft of this annotation said 52 of 52 under an unstated stricter normalisation; the ratio is 100% either way); vllm#53336 restored them unchanged three days later. The consequences are asymmetric and none of them is 'port all three': porting BOTH 229 and 241 is the same work twice; porting 229 WITHOUT 230 is correct and complete for this behaviour; porting 230 WITHOUT 241 REGRESSES 229. Against this tree all three are moot today for a separate reason -- there is no FlashAttention metadata builder at all (`include/vllm/v1/attention/backend.h:427-470` is capability plus `get_kv_cache_shape` only, and `CommonAttentionMetadata` carries no head geometry), and both `FlashAttentionMetadata` and `get_num_attention_heads_from_layers` return 0 across `src/`+`include/`+`tests/` against positive controls `AttentionMetadata` 148 files and `num_heads` 130 files. 229 and 230 belong to PORTQ-6's tranche and 241 to PORTQ-7's, so neither wave could see the chain from inside its own slice. The readings of the UPSTREAM diffs above are accurate and are left as written.

## 5. INVENTORY (356)

Each entry adds a capability this tree has not ported. Every one owes an
annotation in [`../porting-inventory.md`](../porting-inventory.md) with its
`vllm#` reference and a tier. None is ported in this cycle unless a later wave
tiers it T0 or T1 and schedules it.

- `01af92e175` vllm#49811 [Feature][Model Runner V2] Support extract_hidden_states speculation — INVENTORY: config/vllm.py and gpu/model_runner.py only add "extract_hidden_states" to the supported-method lists; the speculator itself is a new unported capability (v1/worker/gpu/spec_decode/extract_hidden_states.py).

- `35efdf6b34` vllm#47288 [Elastic EP] Async preparation — INVENTORY: every ported-file hunk serves elastic EP scale-up and scale-down, a capability we have not ported; the one non-EP-looking change is gated on VLLM_ELASTIC_EP_SCALE_UP_LAUNCH.
- `38a466e7b6` vllm#46789 [DSV4] Implement Sequence Parallelism — INVENTORY: models/deepseek_v4/nvidia/model.py and mtp.py add sp_shard/sp_all_gather/sp_reduce_scatter around attention and MoE under a new _use_sequence_parallel predicate; sequence parallelism is a parallelism capability we have not ported.
- `3986b967b2` vllm#50498 (feat): optionally disable lookup on PD decode — INVENTORY: a new enable_lookup extra-config key and a capacity-only mode in the unported Mooncake store connector, scheduler and worker.
- `3c79b1a8bf` vllm#52016 [Kernel] Add B12X dense linear backends — INVENTORY: kernels/linear/__init__.py registers the new B12x FP8-tensor, FP8-block, MXFP8, MXFP4 and NVFP4 kernels in the backend map and priority lists; the kernels are new and unported.
- `3c8676aebb` vllm#51650 [PP][XPU] Overlap async-scheduling PP sampled-token broadcast with compute — INVENTORY: gpu_model_runner.py makes the pipeline-parallel sampled-token broadcast async_op; the PP sampled-token broadcast path is not ported.
- `3e372c5ff2` vllm#51837 [Bugfix][ROCm] Give KV-first attention blocks their own page in hybrid models — INVENTORY: v1/worker/gpu/attn_utils.py adds a page-first view for KV-first layouts sharing an allocation with Mamba state; a hybrid KV-cache allocation-layout rule we have not ported, worth checking against our own hybrid allocator.
- `3ee2bd1321` vllm#49691 Fix duplicate HunyuanVL image boundary tokens — INVENTORY: hunyuan_vision.py targets the image start, image and image end triple in the prompt replacement; HunyuanVL is unported, so this is the correct prompt-update rule to carry into its port.
- `41729fc53b` vllm#52388 [K3 Perf] Optimize k3 mamba metadata preparation, 6.6-7.6x kernel performance improvement — INVENTORY: v1/worker/mamba_utils.py is purely additive (new Triton get_aligned_state_indices_multi_group_kernel + MambaSpecDecodeGPUContext.compute_aligned_state_indices), a new fused metadata kernel consumed only by the unported Kimi-K3 KDA path.
- `42ab184ea7` vllm#50721 [MRV2] Enable routed-experts capture — INVENTORY: config/vllm.py drops routed-experts capture from the MRV2-unsupported list and gpu/model_runner.py plumbs RoutedExpertsTensors through the output path; the capture capability is not ported.
- `43eaefba5a` vllm#48791 [ModelRunner V2] Enable sequence pooling for embedding and classification models — INVENTORY: gpu/pool/pooling_runner.py, model_runner.py and async_utils.py rework AsyncPoolingOutput to a list-based PoolerOutput with a finished mask; pooling and embedding models in the V2 runner are unported.
- `45273b8dcb` vllm#51408 [1/N] Harden Transformers modelling backend multi-modal path — INVENTORY: the unported Transformers backend adds per-modality field attribution for multi-modality processor outputs.
- `455edc022b` vllm#42963 [ModelRunnerV2] Support prompt embeds — INVENTORY: config/vllm.py drops prompt embeds from the unsupported list and three other ported files add the plumbing; prompt-embeds input is an unported capability.
- `457a5f34db` vllm#50020 [Bugfix][MRV2] Support encoder timing stats in model runner V2 — INVENTORY: EncoderTimingStats moves to v1/worker/utils.py and gpu/model_runner.py gains get_encoder_timing_stats(); per-request encoder timing is an unported observability surface.
- `460c08bc8a` vllm#52786 [LoRA] Add Qwen3-Omni multimodal LoRA support — INVENTORY: qwen3_omni_moe_thinker.py adds SupportsLoRA plus a qkv rename in the audio encoder and its WeightsMapper; multimodal LoRA on an unported omni model.
- `4635cc3e8f` vllm#50383 Shard the K3 Latent-MoE up-projection on large batches — INVENTORY: the unported latent MoE runner introduces a tail-tier selection choosing a sharded up-projection by token count, auto-gated on SM100; a Kimi-K3 capability we have not ported.
- `463aa5e30f` vllm#52606 [ROCm][Perf] Kimi-K3 Fused kernels for KDA prefill — INVENTORY: engine/arg_utils.py gains a "fused" --kda-prefill-backend choice plus CUDA/ROCm-only validation for a ROCm KDA prefill kernel of the unported Kimi-K3 model (reverted again by 592e06f2ae).
- `466855a2bf` vllm#47017 [ROCm] Enable DeepSeek-V4 on gfx11 — INVENTORY: layers/sparse_attn_indexer.py routes gfx11 to the AITER sparse indexer and platforms/rocm.py adds "inc" to the supported quantizations; a new AMD gfx11 arm we have not ported.
- `470297c143` vllm#50980 [HPC Attention Backend] hpc attention backend support bf16 kv cache with fp8 weight — INVENTORY: the unported HPC attention backend accepts a bf16 KV cache and narrows its experts to SM90; a new arm of an unported third-party backend.
- `4719a9b8f5` vllm#51092 [Bugfix][Spec Decode] Fix EAGLE3 DeepSeek draft crash on non-YaRN rope configs — INVENTORY: the unported EAGLE3 DeepSeek draft stops forcing a rope type so the draft inherits the checkpoint's rope parameters; our ported deepseek_v2.py is untouched.
- `47228db84c` vllm#48534 [Bugfix][KV-transfer] MoRIIO: per-layer READ-completion barrier in wait_for_layer_load — INVENTORY: the unported MoRI-IO connector implements a real per-layer barrier and re-keys in-flight transfers; a correctness barrier.
- `48fc2e2797` vllm#50033 feat(grpc): add KV event source discovery — INVENTORY: kv_events.py adds get_publisher_config(), scheduler.py exposes it and engine/__init__.py adds EngineCoreReadyResponse.kv_events_config; the consumer is the unported Rust gRPC control plane.
- `4a6a3272e8` vllm#51157 [Bugfix][Frontend] Let pooling requests set padding — INVENTORY: entrypoints/pooling/base/protocol.py adds a padding request field threaded into TokenizeParams; a new pooling API parameter our frontend does not expose.
- `4ac452ad98` vllm#51485 [Core] Release NCCL communicator memory in sleep mode — INVENTORY: new opt-in capability (--enable-nccl-comm-suspend, default False) adding suspend/resume to base_device_communicator.py, pynccl.py, parallel_state.py, gpu_worker.py and config/model.py; nothing changes when it is off.
- `4e582c5b54` vllm#50326 [PD][Bugfix] Rebase KV lease deadlines onto worker clock — INVENTORY: the unported NIXL connector gains a scheduler_clock wire field and rebases send TTLs onto the local clock.
- `4eef91c03c` vllm#51831 [Model] Support R3 capture with DeepGEMM MegaMoE — INVENTORY: deepseek_v4/nvidia/model.py gains a capture_fn hook and layer_id on MegaMoEExperts feeding the unported routed_experts_capturer.
- `4f1da84eb5` vllm#46516 Enable gfx1250 ROCm architecture — INVENTORY: new AMD arch; rocm.py adds arch predicates, fp8_utils.py adds a gfx1250 fp32-upcast block GEMM fallback, and two files skip aiter there; every existing arch keeps its old dispatch.
- `4f56321d7e` vllm#47773 [ROCm] Cache fp32 upcast of static e8m0 weight scale in AITER scaled_mm — INVENTORY: the unported ROCm scaled-MM kernel moves the scale upcast into process_weights_after_loading; a load-time weight-scale layout decision.
- `4f6885fffc` vllm#53040 [DSV4][Kernel] Fuse shared experts into MegaMoE — INVENTORY: models/deepseek_v4/nvidia/model.py gains a DeepGEMM SM100 fused shared-expert MegaMoE path (weight transforms, symm-buffer keying, capability probe) with VLLM_DISABLE_DSV4_MEGAMOE_SHARED_EXPERT_FUSION as rollback; a new kernel capability we do not have.
- `4f819f801b` vllm#38390 [Model Runner v2] E/P/D disaggregation support — INVENTORY: new encoder-cache disaggregation capability; config/vllm.py adds is_ec_producer_only and is_encoder_only and gpu/block_table.py adds zero-group guards for encoder-only runs.
- `50ba4bc6b2` vllm#49577 [Feature] Mask Replay — INVENTORY: new return_sampling_mask capability threaded through config/model.py, config/vllm.py validation, arg_utils.py, outputs.py, v1/outputs.py, scheduler.py and the MRV2 runner; off by default.
- `50c51682a1` vllm#50275 [Bugfix][EC Connector] Don't stop an encoder-instance request before its images are encoded — INVENTORY: scheduler.py adds an is_encoder_only stop rule and v1/outputs.py emits an empty list instead of [0] for encoder instances; both reachable only through the unported EC connector.
- `5233368dac` vllm#50516 [ROCm][CI] Fall back to lossless Kimi K3 MXFP4 emulation on gfx942 — INVENTORY: adds an EMULATION backend to the ROCm MXFP4 priority list, leaves activations unquantized for weight-only schemes and registers a SITU activation; a new MXFP4 MoE backend arm.
- `52be12cfac` vllm#50569 feat: allow shared expert overlapping for FlashInfer one-sided all-to-all — INVENTORY: the unported shared-experts runner adds a backend to the EPLB-overlap-safe set.
- `52c0e3cb08` vllm#50157 [Kernel] Add support for Flashinfer Mamba SSU algorithm selection — INVENTORY: arg_utils.py adds --mamba-ssu-algorithm selecting an unported FlashInfer SSU dispatch.
- `5369f7b7b8` vllm#49747 [MXFP8][ROCm] Fix MXFP8 MoE backend selection — INVENTORY: the unported AITER MXFP8 expert now refuses configs whose activation or swiglu constants differ; MXFP8 MoE backend-selection rules.
- `541128bdeb` vllm#50301 [KV Offload] Enable single-copy MLA layout for CPUOffloadingSpec — INVENTORY: the unported CPU offload spec replaces a class flag with a shared-region predicate and puts every rank on slot 0 under replicated layout; a single-copy MLA memory layout.
- `541c6d64c1` vllm#52966 [Bugfix][Quantization] Support CT block FP8 with Marlin — INVENTORY: kernels/linear/scaled_mm/marlin.py resolves weight_scale versus weight_scale_inv so compressed-tensors block-FP8 checkpoints run on Marlin; the Marlin scaled-MM kernel is unported.
- `5426311d91` vllm#47896 [Kernel][ROCm][Perf] FlyDSL decode-attention kernel for 4-bit TurboQuant KV cache — INVENTORY: rocm.py only relaxes explicit attention-backend rejection for turboquant KV dtypes; the capability is unported.
- `542a8fad6d` vllm#50094 [KV Offload] Move CPUOffloadingSpec onto SharedOffloadRegion — INVENTORY: backs each worker's CPU buffer with a slot in a shared mmap region; a new CPU-offload allocation backend, unported.
- `555ea65e8c` vllm#54242 [Frontend] Add video embeds input support — INVENTORY: new input capability; entrypoints/chat_utils.py adds video-embeds parsing and models/qwen3_vl.py adds Qwen3VLMultiModalDataParser so frame timestamps travel with out-of-band video embeddings.
- `55aa766dc8` vllm#54052 [Bugfix][Model] Fix GraniteMoeHybrid per-expert quantized weight loading — INVENTORY: granitemoehybrid.py (unported) routes MoE expert names through maybe_remap_moe_expert_param_name; a GraniteMoeHybrid port must carry the remap.
- `55c98e370a` vllm#50661 [Model Runner v2] Enable BGE M3 pooling embed token_classify — INVENTORY: gpu/pool/pooling_runner.py adds the new combined pooling task and its token-task classification; a new pooling capability.
- `56c96b0d91` vllm#48774 [Perf] Tune LL BF16 Router GEMM — INVENTORY: the unported CuTeDSL kernel replaces one tuned table with per-arch SM90 and SM100f tables keyed by shape for the DSV3.2, DSV4 and GLM5.2 router shapes; the dispatch table is nonetheless the reference for our router GEMM.
- `5707355209` vllm#54465 [Bugfix][MLA] Fix BLHNC addressing for FlashInfer sparse MLA — INVENTORY: deepseek_v32/common/kernels.py, mla/flashinfer_mla_sparse.py and mla/sparse_utils.py, none ported; adds BLOCK_STRIDE_ROWS so sparse index conversion stops deriving page size from strides. - `585bb07c70` vllm#51034 feat: add SSE keep-alive comments for idle streaming responses — INVENTORY: new server capability; entrypoints/openai/cli_args.py adds --sse-keep-alive-interval (default 0 = off) and its validation, with the emitter in the unported sse_keep_alive.py.

- `58de6cbdc2` vllm#52929 Add NemotronH_Omni_Reasoning_V3 as a supported Nemotron architecture — INVENTORY: models/registry.py registers a new multimodal architecture and config/speculative.py promotes its text_config for MTP detection; a model we have not ported.

- `59d7fc92ea` vllm#51262 [Bugfix][DeepSeek V4] Handle trailing system messages in prompt rendering — INVENTORY: tokenizers/deepseek_v4_encoding.py and the Rust renderer now emit the Assistant transition after a trailing or pre-assistant system turn; a DSV4 prompt-rendering port must match these token strings.
- `5a4c8d9924` vllm#48850 [Bugfix][LoRA] Add embedding_modules for Qwen3.5 CausalLM — INVENTORY: models/qwen3_5.py declares embedding_modules mapping embed_tokens/lm_head onto the vLLM embedding wrappers; this only enables LoRA on embeddings, a capability we have not ported.
- `5b1e7a812b` vllm#51362 [BugFix][Mooncake] Fix Mooncake saves from sparse Mamba block tables — INVENTORY: mooncake/store/worker.py skips NULL_BLOCK_ID source blocks.
- `5bf0dbd6fd` vllm#51824 vLLM crashes at startup when DeepEP v2 is used with --enforce-eager with TRTLLM Bf16 — INVENTORY: fused_moe/experts/trtllm_bf16_moe.py takes top_k from topk_ids.size(1) rather than self.topk.
- `5c4fe4b17e` vllm#49069 [Bugfix][KV Connector] Propagate EAGLE state across merged Mooncake store groups — INVENTORY: the unported Mooncake coordinator rebuilds group merging so the eagle flag propagates to every group sharing a spec.
- `5d07e268b1` vllm#47514 [Quantization][INC] Add MXFP8 Linear Support — INVENTORY: a new MXFP8 linear quantization scheme with group size 32 and a new packing format; unported.
- `5d8a4cf976` vllm#51647 [ROCm] Pad non-aligned AITER MLA heads — INVENTORY: mla/rocm_aiter_mla.py and rocm_aiter_mla_sparse.py generalise head padding to the next multiple of 16 up to 128 and add native-H24 detection.
- `5df31ea52d` vllm#52795 [Spec Decode] Enable adaptive verification on DSv4 + sm90 — INVENTORY: mla/indexer.py adds the Hopper flattened-device-query-len path and makes the DSA indexer CUDA-graph-ALWAYS under adaptive verification.
- `5df9999fcf` vllm#50656 [Kimi-K3] Add option to shard the shared expert instead of replicating — INVENTORY: envs.py adds a default-off flag for the unported Kimi-K3 sequence-parallel MoE.
- `5eaa70dac5` vllm#51411 [Bugfix][Quantization] Fix INT8 W8A8 MoE crash in TritonExperts — INVENTORY: the unported Triton expert admits int8 hidden states and maps them to a bf16 compute type; an INT8 W8A8 MoE arm.
- `5ec47f3e48` vllm#50234 [PD][PushConnector] Record last activity of remotes to allow clean up of stale ones — INVENTORY: the unported NIXL push worker stamps last-active so push-mode remotes participate in stale eviction.
- `5fa0154448` vllm#49647 [Rubin] Enable NVLink all-reduce paths on SM107 — INVENTORY: adds capability-107 entries to three unported all-reduce tables; new NVIDIA arch enablement for a targeted platform.
- `6014f9e672` vllm#52079 [Kimi-K3] Add GEMM-RS for sequence parallelism — INVENTORY: envs.py adds a default-off flag for an unported SM100 BF16 GEMM-RS kernel.
- `601fa9a74e` vllm#49612 [KV Connector] Support NIXL heterogeneous P/D block sizes for hybrid models — INVENTORY: the unported NIXL worker adds block-size-ratio descriptor expansion with SSM descriptors never expanded, and reworks worker geometry.
- `60915c972c` vllm#47750 [Feature] Add VidCom2 video token pruning — INVENTORY: new vidcom2 video pruning method across config/multimodal.py, config/model.py, interfaces.py and qwen3_vl.py; the default stays evs.
- `61826c1c6f` vllm#51624 [Hardware][Power] Unqualified MoE Backend for Power (VSX) — INVENTORY: a new bf16-only Power VSX CPU MoE expert backed by 445 lines of csrc micro-GEMM; a CPU MoE ISA arm outside our current x86 and ARM targets.
- `61ac368021` vllm#50090 [Kimi-K3] Add AttnRes kernels — INVENTORY: _custom_ops.py adds the kimi_k3_attn_res binding for a new unported CUDA kernel.
- `61c1dd0966` vllm#49579 [EC Connector] Call to EC Connector update_connector_output from scheduler — INVENTORY: scheduler.py forwards ec_connector_output to the unported EC connector; dead for us without that connector.
- `62c5e21621` vllm#50507 [KV Offloading] Support partial-tail prefix reuse with fine-grained prefix matching — INVENTORY: offloading/scheduler.py adds a new partial-tail capability gated on single-block chunks with mamba-align groups; existing store and load fencing is renamed but preserved.
- `6370e53f24` vllm#48145 [Frontend] Reuse prefill token ids on the decode chat path for disaggregated serving — INVENTORY: the unported online renderer reuses prefill token ids from the KV transfer params on the decode side; a disaggregated-serving frontend capability.
- `63ac04a61e` vllm#50484 [Kimi-K3] DCP support — INVENTORY: new MLADCPManager and direct-A2A DCP kernels; the two ported MLA files only change behavior when dcp_world_size > 1, plus a new workspace-size helper that vllm#51733 then fixes.
- `63e78ce365` vllm#49611 [Benchmark] Add probe requests to vllm bench serve — INVENTORY: benchmarks/serve.py adds an opt-in probe-request loop and its metrics; a new benchmark capability, default off.
- `63ff748f65` vllm#46514 [Attention][MLA] FlashMLA sparse: DCP on the fp8_ds_mla mixed-batch path plus MTP — INVENTORY: mla/flashmla.py and flashmla_sparse.py add DCP LSE return, empty-row neutralisation and the gathered-head padding envelope check.
- `640a09086d` vllm#49948 Fix DoS via sample-rate forgery bypassing audio decode duration guard — INVENTORY: the ported change is only a new env var in envs.py; the guard is in the unported audio module, and vllm.cpp has no audio decode duration cap at all.
- `652ba59229` vllm#50574 [Model Runner V2] Enable encoder token embedding — INVENTORY: gpu/pool/pooling_runner.py adds the token_embed task and a LateInteractionRunner with request-keyed reservations; a new pooling capability.
- `653cc6faca` vllm#50620 [Bugfix][NIXL] Include transfer mode (push/pull) in the compatibility hash — INVENTORY: six unported NIXL files thread the transfer mode into the compatibility hash so push and pull peers cannot handshake.
- `657f9b9ce2` vllm#53838 [ROCm][DSV4][Perf] Fuse DeepSeek V4 C4 compressor GEMMs — INVENTORY: models/deepseek_v4/amd/{model,rocm}.py concatenate the main and indexer compressor weights into one GEMM; ROCm is a targeted platform so this is a real owed perf item.
- `65cf1276a2` vllm#50776 [Kernel] Skip fully masked key blocks in windowed Triton prefill — INVENTORY: the unported Triton prefill kernel derives its scan limits from the sliding window so a windowed pass stops costing quadratic time; an algorithmic win worth mirroring in our windowed prefill.
- `674c284dbd` vllm#54056 Fix Humming MoE activation_output aliasing — INVENTORY: fused_moe/experts/fused_humming_moe.py drops activation_output from the buffer chain under fused SITU and FP8 quant so the even/odd workspace two-colouring stops aliasing gate_up_output with quanted_down_input.
- `68fb303a41` vllm#48438 [Bugfix] Preserve Marlin runtime tensor storage across weight reload — INVENTORY: the unported Marlin kernels gain a workspace-reuse argument so a reload reuses the storage a CUDA graph captured.
- `6914d60b1e` vllm#49544 [ROCm][Perf] gfx942: use FlyDSL fp8 MQA logits kernel — INVENTORY: the unported ROCm sparse-MLA backend routes gfx942 to AITER FlyDSL instead of our ported fp8 MQA logits helper; the ported helper's own behavior is unchanged.
- `699e180df4` vllm#53574 [Bugfix][SM120] DSv4: pass contiguous C128A decode topk indices on SM120 — INVENTORY: models/deepseek_v4/sparse_mla.py stops slicing the decode eidx buffer on capability major 12 because the FlashInfer SM120 kernel ignores the row stride; directly relevant to GB10-class hardware.
- `6a5e8f5979` vllm#51398 [DeepEPv2] Support MXFp8 Activation Scale Dispatch — INVENTORY: fused_moe/prepare_finalize/deepep_v2.py adds mxfp8 pre-dispatch quantisation with int32 scale packing and post-dispatch swizzle.
- `6a9109d865` vllm#48420 [Bugfix] Fix Qwen3-Omni crash on video with no audio track — INVENTORY: two unported omni thinkers raise a ValueError instead of asserting when audio-in-video has no audio.
- `6a9c69fa85` vllm#52157 [Attention][Spec Decode] Support varlen trtllm-gen decode for adaptive verification — INVENTORY: v1/attention/backends/flashinfer.py adds the device-qo_indptr varlen decode path gated on SM100 plus trtllm-gen and no DCP.
- `6adad08767` vllm#51655 Add Muse Glimmer model support — INVENTORY: new model muse_glimmer.py (1649 lines) plus its reasoning and tool parsers and a registry entry; it also propagates the target's is_neox_style into the DFlash drafter and relaxes the Eagle3 aux-layer unwrap in interfaces.py. This is the merged squash that discharges deviation 16.
- `6b68db441e` vllm#50803 [ROCm] Fix DeepSeek V4 indexer numerics and coverage — INVENTORY: models/deepseek_v4/common/ops/fused_indexer_q.py adds USE_EXPLICIT_FMA on ROCm to match HIP rotary contraction before the bf16 materialisation; a ROCm-only numerics rule.
- `6c4aa8bec3` vllm#53751 [RL] Support checkpoint-coordinate sparse NCCL weight updates — INVENTORY: distributed/weight_transfer/sparse_nccl_engine.py reworks the update protocol onto checkpoint coordinates.
- `6c7e679f04` vllm#49714 [ROCm][Bugfix] Sanitize AITER paged-MQA logits before sparse top-k for DSV4 — INVENTORY: the unported ROCm sparse backend adds a NaN-to-negative-infinity sanitisation before sparse top-k; a NaN-into-top-k hazard to record for the ROCm DSA arm.
- `6cd9713463` vllm#43375 [RL] P2P RDT weight sync — INVENTORY: new distributed/weight_transfer/sharded_rdt_* subsystem (~3.6k lines) plus a factory entry; an entirely unported capability.
- `6e85feb1b7` vllm#51827 [3/N] Harden Transformers modelling backend multi-modal path — INVENTORY: models/transformers/multimodal.py replaces the mm_token_type_ids placeholder strategy with PromptReplacement-based processors.
- `6ea6c42659` vllm#51997 [Bugfix] Bound Anthropic stop sequences — INVENTORY: the unported Anthropic protocol bounds stop sequences by a new env limit.
- `6ec92bcbc8` vllm#54015 [Kimi-K3] Merge MLA gate into QKV-A projection — INVENTORY: layers/linear.py adds KimiK3MergedQKVGateLinear (replicated latent shards + TP gate shard with its own weight loaders), a new fused-projection capability for the unported Kimi-K3 model.
- `6f00a1ae3b` vllm#42436 fused_moe: add VLLM_TRITON_USE_TD tensor-descriptor path — INVENTORY: fused_moe.py gains an opt-in tensor-descriptor variant of the MoE Triton kernel behind an env flag; the default path is unchanged.
- `702e1d7186` vllm#53361 [LoRA] feat: Support LoRA for DeepSeek V4 — INVENTORY: new LoRA capability for DSV4; routed_experts.py splits the base_layer prefix into checkpoint-side and param-side arguments (identical for non-LoRA), and deepseek_v4/nvidia/model.py adds SupportsLoRA, packed_modules_mapping, lora_skip_prefixes and base_layer name fallbacks in the loader.
- `704f12aa1e` vllm#53213 [Pooling] Report input throughput for batched requests — INVENTORY: benchmark-harness capability; benchmarks/lib/endpoint_request_func.py counts num_input_sequences per pooling/embedding/rerank request and benchmarks/serve.py adds total_input_sequences and input_sequence_throughput to EmbedBenchmarkMetrics and the JSON output.
- `70aaec832b` vllm#52246 [Bugfix][Anthropic] Return 4xx for client-caused errors in /v1/messages — INVENTORY: the unported Anthropic router replaces a blanket 500 with the shared error response.
- `70afdedc10` vllm#51855 [K3] support recoverssm for K3 — INVENTORY: new RecoverSSM speculative-decode capability for Kimi-K3 KDA with a new cache flag, a new validation block, 4-tensor MambaStateShapes and a zero speculative-block count; all new behavior is gated on the new flag.
- `7153fd70f6` vllm#49558 [Bugfix][MoE] Filter packed expert weights during EP loading — INVENTORY: the unported EP weight filter extends its skip test to packed expert weights.
- `7154856f3d` vllm#47791 [Bugfix] Fix handling 5D KV cache in kv_postprocess_layout_on_receive — INVENTORY: the unported KV-transfer utils pick a different inverse order for 5-D caches.
- `7156c63bef` vllm#52980 [SM100] Hdim 256 optimized — INVENTORY: flash_attn.py adds a whole FA4 head_size=256 SM100 kernel path (FA4_HD256_PAGE_SIZE block-size advertising, cascade disable, encoder fallback to FA2); vllm.cpp's FlashAttentionBackend has no FA4 hd256 kernel and its get_supported_kernel_block_sizes() is still {16}.
- `72c0d67657` vllm#46727 [Feat] Support thinking_token_budget in Model Runner V2 — INVENTORY: enables a capability we have not ported; the ported files only lose the unsupported-on-V2 refusal and pass configuration through to new sampler code in unported files.
- `738bc8811a` vllm#50858 [BugFix] Disable TP for Qwen3-Omni audio encoder when heads % TP != 0 — INVENTORY: qwen3_omni_moe_thinker.py sets disable_tp on the 20-head audio encoder projections when TP does not divide the head count.
- `74d3b799e1` vllm#49429 [Bugfix] Fix mHC block-M prenorm GEMM cross-row reduction carry-over — INVENTORY: the unported mHC tilelang kernels move scalar accumulators to per-row local arrays.
- `7553aac77b` vllm#51906 [Frontend] Add routed-experts prompt offset — INVENTORY: a new request and sampling field plus a new DCP/PCP incompatibility check; serving.py merely moves base64 encoding to a shared helper.
- `75dde08d3f` vllm#51114 [Perf][MoE] Optimize deepep_v2 receiver CPU Overhead — INVENTORY: the unported DeepEP-v2 prepare/finalize replaces a per-expert host loop with a cached vectorised form.
- `76ff0cdff2` vllm#53821 [Bugfix][ROCm] Preserve AITER unified-attention metadata during graph replay — INVENTORY: v1/attention/backends/rocm_aiter_unified_attn.py gets its own builder that fills seq_lens with 1 at capture while preserving query_start_loc.
- `7743486190` vllm#50580 [Frontend] DeepSeek V4 0731 reasoning effort prompts and mappings — INVENTORY: the unported DSV4 tokenizer makes thinking default on, remaps reasoning effort and adds a per-level preamble; this shifts rendered DSV4 prompts, so any DSV4 token gate must track it.
- `77469c9057` vllm#50476 [ROCm][MLA] Mask the AITER MLA small-head verify flatten causally — INVENTORY: the unported ROCm MLA backend gives each verify row its own KV window; our ported triton_mla flatten is gated on the non-causal DSpark block, so the same defect is not present there.
- `777b01d1a8` vllm#45254 [MM][CG] Support ViT full CUDA graph for Ernie-4.5-VL image inference — INVENTORY: an unported model gains encoder CUDA-graph support and a metadata split.
- `7797b6022c` vllm#53247 [Kernel][Perf] Per-architecture tuned configs for batch-invariant persistent matmul — INVENTORY: new batch_invariant_configs.py plus a hook in batch_invariant.py; the batch-invariant determinism mode is not ported.
- `77cba0259f` vllm#48123 [KV Offloading] Per-request tier filtering with TierFilter/TierMatcher — INVENTORY: kv_offload/base.py adds the filter types and a per-request load_tier_filter, and kv_events.py collapses two media enums into one; a new capability rather than a fix to ported behavior.
- `7a67941c1d` vllm#53760 [Rust Frontend][gRPC] Add audio and video media inputs — INVENTORY: the Rust gRPC frontend accepts audio and video media items and adds a structural-prefix placeholder range; qwen3_asr.py switches M-RoPE audio length to mm_position.length.
- `7aea73d83d` vllm#49348 [ROCm][Quark][6/N] Use MXFP4 linear kernel abstraction for aiter backend — INVENTORY: kernels/linear/__init__.py registers AiterMxfp4LinearKernel for ROCm and in the aiter backend map; a targeted-platform kernel we have not ported.
- `7b7e5cbfcd` vllm#53519 [Bugfix][LoRA] Restore tower and connector LoRA support for LFM2-VL — INVENTORY: lfm2_vl.py re-declares supports_tower_connector_lora = True.
- `7bbbf7c8e5` vllm#51251 [Core] Configure custom encoder cache managers from VllmConfig — INVENTORY: adds --ec-manager-config and an EncoderCacheManager factory that scheduler.py routes through; the default manager choice is unchanged.
- `7bc1660bd6` vllm#51931 [Misc] Use VLLMValidationError in pooling input validation — INVENTORY: an unported pooling IO processor changes the HTTP status class for validation failures.
- `7c5dc571cb` vllm#51724 [Attention][DSA] Enable W4A16 DSA — INVENTORY: adds a new KV cache dtype "nvfp4_ds_mla" (352-byte opaque per-token blob) with a new KVQuantMode, a new cp_gather_and_upconvert_nvfp4_kv_cache op and MLA spec/validation plumbing in cache.py, vllm.py, mla_attention.py, torch_utils.py, kv_cache_interface.py; a KV quantization format vllm.cpp does not have.
- `7c6729b769` vllm#50089 [Model] Add Kimi K3 support: model files and kernels [1/N] — INVENTORY: new model plus new ops, a SituAndMul activation in activation.py, K3 custom ops and env entries, and an optional out argument on causal_conv1d_update.
- `7c877062ac` vllm#50572 [kernel] Integrate FlashInfer BF16 CuTeDSL Low Latency GEMM — INVENTORY: layers/utils.py adds the cuda_flashinfer_bf16_gemm custom op and its SM100 eligibility predicate, linear.py caches dispatch_unquantized_gemm(linear_backend) per method, and kernels/linear/__init__.py turns the backend filter from error-on-empty into warn-and-fall-back-to-auto; vllm.cpp has no --linear-backend kernel-set filter and no CuTeDSL GEMM.
- `7cab4368f2` vllm#50929 [MM][CG] Support ViT full CUDA graph for Kimi-K2.5 — INVENTORY: an unported model implements the encoder CUDA-graph protocol with padded replay buffers.
- `7cfb97e337` vllm#48915 [Frontend][Core][Spec Decode] Per-request acceptance stats in OpenAI API responses — INVENTORY: new opt-in flag --per-request-spec-decode-metrics with a new RequestSpecDecodeMetrics type threaded through config/vllm.py, arg_utils.py, outputs.py, scheduler.py, v1/engine, metrics/stats.py and both OpenAI protocol/serving pairs; a frontend capability we have not ported.
- `7d5769b2d9` vllm#53685 [Perf][DSv4] Use native CUDA SwiGLU clamp kernel for Humming MoE — INVENTORY: fused_moe/utils.py routes the non-pad-aware clamped SwiGLU to torch.ops._C.silu_and_mul_with_clamp on CUDA; that file is not ported.
- `7de96050c5` vllm#52783 [Spec Decode] Enable adaptive DSpark on SM100 sparse MLA — INVENTORY: gpu/model_runner.py computes target_attn_layer_names and passes vllm_config/target_layer_names/additional_attn_cg_support into maybe_create_adaptive_verification_manager; adaptive verification is absent from vllm.cpp.
- `7ea4b40954` vllm#52502 [Hardware][NVIDIA] Add GB10 fused-MoE fp8 tuning configs (E=256, E=512) — INVENTORY: two new NVIDIA GB10 fused-MoE fp8 block-[128,128] tuning tables; measured GB10 tile, warp and stage choices, directly usable as tuning reference for our own GB10 MoE fp8 arm.
- `7f4793eaa3` vllm#50611 [Nixl][PD] DCP support for MLA models — INVENTORY: new PD+DCP capability; kv_transfer.py gains has_connector(), config/vllm.py adds NIXL DCP asserts and adjust_dcp_kv_cache_interleave_size(), sparse_attn_indexer.py defers cp_kv_cache_interleave_size behind a lazy property, and mla_attention.py drops its eager copy; all of it is gated on a configured KV connector.
- `7f7a32cfec` vllm#47808 [Spec Decode] DSpark confidence-scheduled verification — INVENTORY: new adaptive-verification capability with a new sampling parameter, new env vars, a new backend predicate and on-device draft trimming across three ported runner files.
- `7fbfca2670` vllm#52529 [Bugfix][Frontend] Only echo the assistant turn in batched chat completions — INVENTORY: entrypoints/openai/chat_completion/batch_serving.py now requires conversation[-1]["role"] == role before echoing; batch_serving.py is not ported although the non-batch serving.py is.
- `8040ef2426` vllm#49754 [Frontend] expose stream_interval as req sampling param — INVENTORY: a new per-request stream_interval field with its own validation in sampling_params.py and a clamp in output_processor.py; a new streaming capability.
- `8060260e1d` vllm#49932 [Linear][Kernel] add block-wise scaled_mm — INVENTORY: kernels/linear/__init__.py registers a new block-wise Torch FP8 scaled-MM kernel in the pytorch backend map and the CUDA fp8-block candidate list; existing priorities unchanged.
- `8061dc26bd` vllm#49392 [Bugfix] Normalize sparse MLA warmup compression ratios — INVENTORY: the unported DSA indexer backend clamps compression ratios in the warmup dispatch trace.
- `8170c23c4f` vllm#49764 [Quantization] Share online weight scales across TP — INVENTORY: quant_utils.py adds weight_amax and two all-reduce helpers for the online-quantization path; the callers live in unported modules.
- `81840a172f` vllm#48414 [KV Connector] Canonical CPU layout for parallelism-agnostic KV offload — INVENTORY: kv_offload/cpu/gpu_worker.py adds a fragment-copy engine for the new canonical CPU layout and file_mapper.py adds canonical_format to the storage namespace; a new offload layout capability.
- `81962bb699` vllm#49043 [Bugfix] Reject invalid FlashInfer MNNVL workspaces — INVENTORY: the unported FlashInfer all-reduce destroys and rejects a workspace with no multicast pointer instead of using it.
- `81a42d37b1` vllm#49498 [Frontend] Add cache_salt support to Anthropic Messages API — INVENTORY: the unported Anthropic surface adds the field and forwards it into prefix-cache salting.
- `82553692b8` vllm#50153 [KV Connector] Fix NIXL mamba state pairing for multi-slot block tables — INVENTORY: two unported NIXL files replace the clipping helper with one that strips SSM speculative scratch and non-positional state slots.
- `82642d7d6c` vllm#49750 [Perf] RMSNorm uncontiguous support, 1.2 to 3.1x kernel performance improvement — INVENTORY: the capability is a strided-input RMSNorm CUDA kernel in csrc, not ported; deepseek_v2.py and minicpm3.py only drop the contiguous() copies that kernel makes unnecessary.
- `827a2af806` vllm#48666 [Kernel] Gemma-4 FA4 FP8 Kernel — INVENTORY: new SM90 FA4 FP8-KV kernel arm; v1/attention/backend.py and platforms/interface.py gain config-aware block-size accessors that are behavior-neutral without the kernel.
- `82b7d49a6e` vllm#51217 [MoE] Generalize masked activation for padded layouts — INVENTORY: csrc activation kernels and four unported expert modules gain masked activation over padded expert layouts.
- `831d3848f1` vllm#48879 [Core] Fail fast when /dev/shm is too small for the shm ring buffer — INVENTORY: the unported shm broadcast adds a free-space check before creating the segment; a new startup validation error.
- `837eae6458` vllm#50298 [DSv4 Perf] Remove redundant full kernel for dsv4, 1.88x kernel performance improvement — INVENTORY: two unported DSV4 files give the top-k combine an output workspace so the per-chunk fill disappears; a real DSV4 sparse-prefill lever.
- `8543522ca7` vllm#51007 [KV Offload] Support out-of-tree secondary tier managers via module_path — INVENTORY: two unported tiering files resolve an unregistered tier type from a module path; new plugin extensibility on a subtree we partly mirror.
- `85c1365bd9` vllm#53790 [Bugfix] NemotronHMTP: add hf_to_vllm_mapper so quant exclusions reach the MTP draft — INVENTORY: nemotron_h_mtp.py adds the mapper so SupportsQuant re-roots exclude_modules and the MTP experts stop being wrongly quantized.
- `86e2ab50aa` vllm#51120 [Bugfix][Frontend] Return 400 for invalid PyNvVideoCodec video input — INVENTORY: the unported video module maps decoder exceptions to ValueError so a bad video is a 400 instead of a 500.
- `872fd5973e` vllm#51002 [Bugfix][LoRA] Guard TrtLlm BF16 MoE LoRA gate on activation type — INVENTORY: the unported MoE oracle replaces three partial checks with the full support test including activation format.
- `882ca8d696` vllm#53014 [Kernel] add Flashinfer cutedsl w4a16 linear — INVENTORY: adds FlashInferCuteDslNvFp4W4A16LinearKernel to the NVFP4 candidate lists and makes it the forced a16 kernel on SM100/103; it flips the very selection rule vllm.cpp cites in dense_nvfp4_gemm.h ("auto and use_a16 gives MarlinNvFp4LinearKernel"), but the kernel itself is unported so it cannot be followed yet.
- `88e1b11313` vllm#53606 [Perf] Tune FlashInfer all-reduce thresholds for single-node TP8 on SM103 — INVENTORY: allreduce_rms_fusion.py and all_reduce_utils.py raise the 103/TP8 fusion cap to 4MB and add the (103, 8, 1) MNNVL entry; a tuning-table item.
- `89ac407e3d` vllm#50716 [Perf] Speed up multimodal placeholder and token-match scanning — INVENTORY: the unported processing/processor.py gets an index-based fast scan, a per-target first-match cache and lazily resolved placeholder candidates, plus one new validation error.
- `89c8401c8a` vllm#52037 [Model] Skip unused Jina V5 output layers — INVENTORY: an unported model wraps init so the logits processor and LM head become missing-stage layers for the pooling-only variant.
- `8ae8337ffa` vllm#50911 [Spec Decode] Enable fused non-causal TokenSpeed MLA for DSpark — INVENTORY: the unported TokenSpeed MLA declares non-causal multi-token decode support and passes a causal mask, so a DSpark block stays fused instead of being flattened.
- `8bcc916a98` vllm#51727 [Bugfix] Fix DeepSeek V4/3.2 tokenizer vocab size overcount crashing guided decoding — INVENTORY: two unported tokenizers drop a length override that double-counted added tokens.
- `8c2bbe00d5` vllm#53513 [Bugfix][LoRA] Add multimodal module mapping for Muse-Glimmer — INVENTORY: muse_glimmer.py gains get_mm_mapping() returning MultiModelKeys; vllm.cpp has no MultiModelKeys and only a minimal LoRA surface.
- `8e1131e1d1` vllm#52114 [Model][Quantization] Add Ling hybrid MXFP4 routed experts support — INVENTORY: two unported models add an MXFP4 routed-expert quant method, scale detection and a name remap; a new quantized arm.
- `8f03625b3d` vllm#44834 [CPU][Zen] Route Int8 MoE inference through zentorch on AMD — INVENTORY: new ZenCPUExpertsInt8 in fused_moe/experts/cpu_moe.py plus oracle and compressed-tensors wiring; CPU is a targeted platform, so this is an owed CPU W8A8 MoE backend.
- `8f158d0ee2` vllm#44359 [MoE] Share apply_moe_activation support metadata — INVENTORY: the unported activation module introduces a shared activation config with quant-over-MoE precedence and backend-gating metadata; the ported fused_moe.py is untouched.
- `8fe9317f2e` vllm#49636 [Model][MoE] DeepSeek-V4: add opt-in FlashInfer moe_ep expert backend — INVENTORY: deepseek_v4/nvidia/model.py generalizes moe_backend == "deep_gemm_mega_moe" to MEGA_MOE_BACKENDS and routes to a new DeepseekV4MegaMoEExpertsFI; a new opt-in expert backend we do not have.
- `8fea1d306f` vllm#43680 Fix uniform_random routing simulation to sample without replacement — INVENTORY: the unported routing simulator replaces randint with rand plus topk so simulated ids are distinct.
- `9069a57139` vllm#49040 [Core][Frontend] Add weight version tagging for RL rollouts — INVENTORY: five ported files add get, set and update weight-version APIs for RL weight transfer.
- `91a893de64` vllm#52809 [Bugfix][Spec Decode] Scope DSpark backend inheritance to DeepSeek V4 — INVENTORY: v1/worker/gpu/spec_decode/dspark/utils.py only inherits the target's attention backend when model_type == "deepseek_v4"; relevant to our DSpark block-drafter work.
- `92bdee05cb` vllm#52399 [Bugfix][Frontend] Return all choices from /inference/v1/generate when n > 1 — INVENTORY: entrypoints/scale_out/token_in_token_out/serving.py sets output_kind FINAL_ONLY for non-streaming so all n choices survive.
- `92ccd2c306` vllm#47237 [Bugifx][INC] Fix INC quantization method selection for non-quantized layers — INVENTORY: quantization/inc/inc.py returns None instead of UnquantizedLinearMethod for layers that are neither LinearBase/ParallelLMHead nor RoutedExperts.
- `9409f59e09` vllm#52514 [Core] Add CuMemAllocator.discard() for tag-selective GPU memory release — INVENTORY: device_allocator/__init__.py adds discard to the MemAllocator protocol; the capability lives in the unported cumem allocator.
- `948107acf7` vllm#48589 [Bugfix] Enhance extra_config handling for layer name suffix matching — INVENTORY: the unported INC config parser makes extra-config keys suffix-match fully qualified layer names.
- `952694e384` vllm#49230 [Bugfix] Validate NIXL speculative config compatibility — INVENTORY: the unported NIXL metadata folds speculative factors into the compatibility hash and bumps the connector version.
- `95901ce70a` vllm#51823 fix(pooling): validate BGE-M3 combined task ownership — INVENTORY: two unported pooling files register an unsupported-combined-task processor so the combined task is refused outside a plugin request.
- `95c9144424` vllm#42662 [LoRA][Gemma4] Support vision tower LoRA — INVENTORY: models/interfaces.py adds get_mm_lora_token_counts with a default reproducing today's counts and gpu_model_runner.py consumes it per modality.
- `96acd473a7` vllm#52122 [Bugfix][MiniCPM-V] Fix AssertionError in get_dummy_mm_data — INVENTORY: an unported model converts video dummy options into per-frame image options.
- `96add737ff` vllm#50641 [Elastic EP] Fix non-contiguous weight transfers — INVENTORY: the unported elastic-EP execute makes each parameter contiguous before the peer-to-peer op because PyNccl ignores strides, and copies back on receive.
- `96fa3f42c9` vllm#49659 [Perf] Skip ll_bf16 router GEMM warmup for non-MoE models — INVENTORY: the unported kernel warmup gates the router-GEMM warmup on the model being MoE; a startup-latency lever.
- `9833aa53d5` vllm#50358 [Bugfix] Fail fast when CPU offload region exceeds available space — INVENTORY: the unported shared offload region adds a free-space precheck plus creator-path cleanup; vllm.cpp has no shared-mmap offload region.
- `98e91a9600` vllm#49345 [PD][NixlPush] Skip extra add_remote_agent step in D-to-P handshake — INVENTORY: the unported NIXL push worker always passes notif-agents-only, a real handshake-cost change.
- `98f86b9c02` vllm#50017 [ROCm][bugfix] Chunked prefill paged decode masked load perf — INVENTORY: the unported Triton paged-decode op restricts the per-token NaN mask to the final tile; same numerics, cheaper loads.
- `99013d77d3` vllm#53253 [Bugfix][Distributed] Gate cross-node MNNVL custom all-reduce by group capability — INVENTORY: device_communicators/custom_all_reduce.py adds a collective MIN vote on local multicast support before entering the cross-node path; that file is not ported.
- `99de48e98f` vllm#49982 Fix MLA padding and grouped topk routing in the Transformers modelling backend — INVENTORY: four unported files add expanded-MLA value padding, grouped-topk routing with a correction bias, and a router dtype; the auto-backend is unported.
- `99e62b802c` vllm#49519 [Bugfix][Model Loader] Defer post-load attention weight processing — INVENTORY: the unported layerwise reload loader generalizes the deferral predicate to any attention layer base.
- `9a4fd57cac` vllm#50688 [Model] Support jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano (EuroBERT encoder backbone) — INVENTORY: a new model arm in an unported file plus a non-causal config setting.
- `9ae11a6b89` vllm#50524 [Model] Add K-EXAONE-2.0-750B-A37B — INVENTORY: new model; registry.py only corrects the arch key spelling and the model code lands in unported files.
- `9b0ab5dd53` vllm#51216 [ROCm][AMD] Enable preshuffled sparse indexing for 16-token blocks — INVENTORY: two unported ROCm files widen the supported kernel block sizes to any multiple of 16; new KV block granularity.
- `9b2aef5124` vllm#48608 [Bugfix] Video loading: sample over presentable frames, not header sample count — INVENTORY: multimodal/video_decoders/{opencv,pyav}.py correct total_frames_num against the edit-list-aware timeline; a real frame-sampling rule for our multimodal priority.
- `9b5f3454f2` vllm#52313 [LoRA] Avoid false target matches for unsupported module types — INVENTORY: lora/model_manager.py and lora/utils.py replace regex suffix matching with exact-suffix plus runtime isinstance checks; those two files are not ported.
- `9c110fa522` vllm#47189 [Frontend] Cohere chat v2 api support — INVENTORY: new frontend surface; config/model.py adds a tokenizer mode, envs.py a flag, cli_args.py two Cohere-only options and the chat protocol the citation plumbing.
- `9c22668436` vllm#50029 [Quantization] Preserve precision in online NVFP4 expert packing — INVENTORY: the unported online NVFP4 module stops folding the fp32 global scale into bf16 before quantization, removing a rounding step, and quantizes per expert.
- `9d1aa4dda8` vllm#48543 [Frontend] Add diarized_json support for MOSS-Transcribe-Diarize — INVENTORY: models/interfaces.py adds the diarized-transcription types and predicate and models/utils.py the marker and speaker parsers.
- `9e50e1037e` vllm#49857 [Bugfix][CuMem] Make KV-cache wake cleanup tag-safe — INVENTORY: the unported CuMem allocator adds a collect and empty-cache before the tagged wake walk; the sleep and wake allocator has no vllm.cpp counterpart.
- `9e6be4a72b` vllm#50365 [Perf][Sparse MLA] Drop the atomic contention in the index remap — INVENTORY: the unported sparse_utils.py adds a single-tile path with an in-register reduction and a plain store to the DSA index remap.
- `9f3169960a` vllm#50942 [MoE] Align TRTLLM MXFP4 autotune buckets — INVENTORY: the unported TRTLLM MXFP4 experts change the autotune bucket they compile for.
- `a07fac758f` vllm#48442 [Perf] Zero-copy torch.Tensor pickling in shm_broadcast MessageQueue — INVENTORY: the unported shm broadcast adds a reducer emitting CPU tensors as out-of-band buffers.
- `a123159f7a` vllm#48798 Add tiering offloading metrics — INVENTORY: four ported kv_offload files add read and write byte and time counters, hit and query counters, failure and active-job counters, and rename JobMetadata to TransferJob.
- `a1c5b1fd9f` vllm#46701 [Core][V1] Support trace_decode_token_ids for deterministic decode replay — INVENTORY: new opt-in feature (enable_trace_replay in config/model.py, SamplingParams.trace_decode_token_ids + _validate_trace_replay, input_processor normalization); a capability vllm.cpp has not ported.
- `a2257f95b7` vllm#51885 [Elastic EP] Reduce eager-mode reconfiguration downtime — INVENTORY: distributed/parallel_state.py _replace_active_groups now returns the old groups for the caller to destroy, and the EPLB mapping update replaces re-construction; confined to elastic EP/EPLB, which vllm.cpp does not implement.
- `a311916a29` vllm#46849 [MRV2][Spec] Fuse AR speculator multi-step decodes back into one CUDA graph — INVENTORY: attention/backend.py adds a draft-decode metadata-update capability that three backends implement and the autoregressive speculator gates on, with a rebuild fallback.
- `a3561ef8e4` vllm#52874 [Bugfix][Model] Mistral3: fix image placeholder grid for processor size overrides — INVENTORY: mistral3.py and lightonocr.py derive the patch grid from the processor longest_edge and round to patch size before the spatial merge.
- `a367cdbca0` vllm#48789 [Profiler] Add minimal Triton Proton profiling backend — INVENTORY: config/vllm.py validates the new profiler and gpu_worker.py wires its wrapper.
- `a556f3fccb` vllm#53308 Forward Anthropic vllm_xargs to sampling params — INVENTORY: entrypoints/anthropic/{protocol,serving}.py accept and forward vllm_xargs.
- `a5f82882a6` vllm#53830 [Bugfix][Model] Honor Molmo2 dummy video num_frames >= 2 override — INVENTORY: molmo2.py clamps the dummy video frame count to at least 2.
- `a60c66e3dc` vllm#53034 [Bugfix] Fix int32 index overflow in LoRA punica kernels at long context — INVENTORY: lora/ops/triton_ops/kernel_utils.py casts row offsets to int64 in the expand and shrink kernels; our arm is torch_ops/lora_ops.py and punica_cpu.py.
- `a671679e9f` vllm#51413 [MRv2 Feature] MR v2 weight offloading support — INVENTORY: gpu/model_runner.py only creates and installs the offloader from offload_config; the offloader itself is unported.
- `a7195188a4` vllm#53121 Add MTP support for Nemotron VL models — INVENTORY: v1/spec_decode/llm_base_proposer.py adds a NemotronH_Nano_VL_V2 branch for image_token_index; a model vllm.cpp has not ported.
- `a758a9f671` vllm#54293 [Perf][KV Connector] Pin token_indices before non_blocking H2D in hf3fs helper — INVENTORY: hf3fs/utils/gather_scatter_helper.py switches to async_tensor_h2d so the non-blocking copy is actually from pinned memory.
- `ab98034d4c` vllm#50420 [Frontend][Bugfix] Use default tool call IDs for Kimi K3 — INVENTORY: the unported K3 tool parser stops synthesising ids and lets the tool-call type generate a unique default.
- `ac2ae8798c` vllm#45802 [Frontend] Support count_reasoning_tokens in the Streaming Parser Engine — INVENTORY: parser/abstract_parser.py adds the capability and four engine modules carry per-token counts through so chat serving can report them in usage.
- `acb0f1dcdb` vllm#52288 [Bugfix][Spec Decode] DSpark: inherit the target's attention backend — INVENTORY: the unported dspark utils fall back to the global attention backend when the speculative one is None; our ported dspark speculator resolves no draft backend of its own.

- `f84df12c6a` vllm#48413 [Bugfix][MM] Fix MiniCPM-V placeholder replacement and image processor loading on Transformers v5 — INVENTORY: an additive multimodal-processing capability encoding each segment separately so BPE cannot merge across a placeholder boundary, plus dynamic image-processor resolution; both files unported.
- `f9a0f629bd` vllm#51879 [KV Offload] Expose data-parallel topology to offloading backends — INVENTORY: a new config surface adding data-parallel size and local rank to the offloading parallel config; the two files are unported and our ported offloading scheduler and kv_offload/base.py are untouched.
- `fa9d67f782` vllm#49585 [EC Connector] Added Build Connector Worker Meta for EC Connector — INVENTORY: extends the unported encoder-cache transfer connector; ported v1/outputs.py gains ec_connector_worker_meta helpers, executor/abstract.py an ECOutputAggregator, and gpu/model_runner.py the no-forward merge path.
- `fd859d2e06` vllm#44956 [KV Connector][Mooncake] Add store group semantics — INVENTORY: a new group-id capability keyed per prefix chunk, with capability probing of the replicate config, in the unported Mooncake store worker.
- `fdaa0d9e59` vllm#49331 [ModelRunner V2] Support encoder-only attention — INVENTORY: new encoder-only attention support in the V2 runner (new model_states/encoder_only.py, unported); ported gpu/block_table.py only guards the fused write for the zero-group case.
- `fdab2b10bc` vllm#52425 [ModelRunner v2] Support Transformers pooling model — INVENTORY: encoder-only detection moves from an isinstance scan to an attention-type test over the config's layers, so Transformers pooling models route to the encoder-only state; the file is unported.
- `fe76112ff2` vllm#52882 [ROCm][Perf] Optimize DeepSeek V4 C4A top-k with AITER — INVENTORY: layers/sparse_attn_indexer.py and deepseek_v4/attention.py only thread a new compress_ratio argument into the ROCm AITER sparse path, an AITER kernel we have not ported.
- `ffc4f08c8e` vllm#46116 [Core][KV-transfer] MoRIIO: heterogeneous TP-to-DP prefill/decode read routing — INVENTORY: a new capability carrying the remote DP rank in the transfer params and an explicit remote TP rank in the handshake; the connector is unported.

## 6. IGNORE (406)

Each entry touches a mirrored path and is irrelevant to this tree. The reason is
recorded per entry, as the guide requires.

## 7. Recorded deviations this advance conflicts with

Four deviations in [`../porting-inventory.md`](../porting-inventory.md) §9
conflict with this advance. Three are the same shape: they port from a post-pin
upstream head because at the pin the feature does not exist, and **each names the
sync cycle as its own discharge condition**. The complete history now shows the
exact merge commit for all three, which the first pass could not.

| Deviation | Cited anchor | Merged upstream as | Ancestor of target |
|---|---|---|---|
| 16, Muse Glimmer | `vllm#51655` @ `075d645af6` (branch head, unmerged) | `6adad08767` `Add Muse Glimmer model support (#51655)`, 2026-08-14 | yes |
| 17 (second numbering), Qwen3.5 text-only | `vllm#50210` @ `ad5d29db7` | `ad5d29db70` `[Model] Support Qwen3.5 text-only dense and MoE models (#50210)`, 2026-07-29 | **yes** |
| 18 (second numbering), `dots3_note` | `vllm#51255` @ `origin/main` | `9035151d6c` `[Model] Add native Dots3 NOTE multimodal support (#51255)`, 2026-08-12 | yes |

Every registry key is absent at the pin and present exactly once at the target:

```console
$ git show cdefd9d499:vllm/model_executor/models/registry.py | grep -c '"<key>":'
```

| Key | at pin | at target |
|---|---:|---:|
| `MuseGlimmerForCausalLM` | 0 | 1 |
| `Qwen3_5ForCausalLM` | 0 | 1 |
| `Qwen3_5MoeForCausalLM` | 0 | 1 |
| `Dots3NoteForCausalLM` | 0 | 1 |
| `Qwen4ExpForCausalLM` | 0 | 1 |

**Deviation 16, Muse Glimmer.** Its text says the only upstream implementation is
`vllm#51655`, OPEN and approved but unmerged with 3 of 20 CI checks red, and that
"the exception is discharged by #51655 merging plus a pin advance that includes
it". **#51655 merged on 2026-08-14 as `6adad08767`.** The first half of the
discharge condition is met and the second half is what this cycle prepares.
Three consequences the deviation calls binding change when the pin moves:

- (a) "the anchor is mutable, a force-push or review round on #51655 rewrites
  what we cite" is discharged. A merged squash is immutable.
- (b) "upstream's own gates have NOT fully passed, so where our HF-reference gate
  disagrees with #51655 the HF reference wins" is discharged. The merged commit
  passed upstream's gates, and under AGENTS.md vLLM then outranks the HF
  reference.
- (c) "no speed axis is claimable for this model, the pinned oracle cannot load
  `muse_glimmer` at all" stays **true at the pin** and becomes **false at the
  target**. It must be rewritten with that distinction, not deleted.

Upstream kept working on the file after merging: 6 commits touch
`muse_glimmer.py` between `6adad08767` and the target, +54/-30. Our port is
anchored on the unmerged branch head, so it is behind the merged content by that
much plus whatever the merge itself changed against `075d645af6`.

**Deviation 17 (second numbering), Qwen3.5 text-only.** Its text says the arms
arrived in `vllm#50210` at `ad5d29db7`, and that "this row does not advance the
pin and reconciles nothing else in the `555967922..ad5d29db7` range; the next
upstream-sync cycle reconciles it deliberately". The anchor is a real, reachable
commit dated 2026-07-29. This is the cycle that reconciles it. 8 commits touch
`qwen3_5.py` after `ad5d29db70`, so the anchor is behind the target. Consequence
(b) is unaffected: the 2.4T checkpoint still does not fit GB10.

**Deviation 18 (second numbering), `dots3_note`.** Its text says the architecture
arrived in `vllm#51255`, that the row "advances nothing in `555967922..main`",
and that upstream is STILL MOVING there so a change re-reads its anchors rather
than trusting a cited line. **That warning is correct and is still live**: 8
commits touch `vllm/models/dots3_note/` after `9035151d6c`. Two of them are in
this report's PORT-NOW list. All three W-wave anchor revisions `c205726108`,
`185cada36b` and `06ecec7a84` are ancestors of the target, so the anchors are
reachable, but W3's re-read predates the most recent change.

**Deviation 1, the compute layer.** No conflict, and no edit owed. It replaces
torch, `torch.compile`, Triton and Inductor with `vt::`, and it is what makes the
large IGNORE list defensible: most IGNORE entries are `torch.compile`, Inductor,
CUDA-graph-capture, CPython reference-lifetime or Triton-autotune changes with no
C++ analogue.

**Not a §9 deviation, but the same obligation.** The `qwen4_exp` lane exception
in [`../oracles/transformers.md`](../oracles/transformers.md) named its own
expiry, "it expires the moment vLLM registers `qwen4_exp`". `e126687a9a`
vllm#53896 registered it on 2026-08-31.
[#2489](https://github.com/mudler/vllm.cpp/issues/2489) owns the repair. One
further commit has already landed on that tree, `191cecd51e` vllm#54560, a Hopper
low-latency GEMM tuning table.

**No deviation is edited in this report**, because this cycle does not advance
the pin. The cycle that does advance it cannot do so without settling all four,
which is what the guide means by requiring the deviation doc to describe current
truth in the same cycle.

**Sixteen cited paths exist at neither the pin nor the target.** The first pass

they cannot be verified against anything". The complete history separates them,
and this is correction 3 of §2:

*Group A, our own deviations, correctly absent upstream (4).*

*Group B, stale-but-real anchors: upstream had them and removed them before the

The four `fla/ops/*` anchors matter most: `vllm#48500` moved FLA to a third-party
vendor directory, so our GDN kernels cite paths whose semantics now live
somewhere else entirely.

*Group C, genuinely wrong anchors (3).* Upstream never had these paths, and none
is one of our deviations:

- `vllm/tests/kernels/attention/test_mla_decode_cpu.py`. Upstream tests live at
  `tests/`, not under `vllm/`. The intended path is
  `tests/kernels/attention/test_mla_decode_cpu.py`.
- `vllm/multimodal/media.py`. `vllm/multimodal/media` is a **directory** at the
  target, not a module. The intended path is one of `media/audio.py`,
